Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the superconductors structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 758. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 959 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by superconductors generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 959. QED.
